### story mentor 1

Levels 10–50 · 23 lines · about 3 min · roles: A (NoLifer), B (Leveler/Explorer/Socialite), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer)

- **B:** "big ty to {name_a} for everything. taught me to pull, to bind, to not run into walls" / "shoutout to {name_a}, best teacher in the realm. i was hopeless before"
- **A:** "aw. you did the work {name_b}. i just yelled "dont pull that" a lot" / "lol you were never hopeless {name_b}. just loud"
- **C:** "wait, {name_a} has a student? since when" / "{name_a} is teaching now? since when"
- **B:** "since i was a nobody dying to spiders outside town" / "since my first day, when i died to everything with legs"
- **A:** "i found {name_b} face down outside town. twice in one hour" / "nine deaths in one hour. i counted every one"
- **D:** "thats so nice. nobody ever taught me anything" / "aww. i had to learn everything by dying"
- **F:** "dying is a great teacher. just slow and expensive" / "i learned from dying too. still learning"
- **B:** "and now im {level}. still cant believe it" / "{level} already. all thanks to {name_a}"
- **G:** "good teachers are rare. hold on to that one {name_b}" / "{name_a} has helped half the realm. you got lucky"
- **B:** "oh and big news. that big rvr group asked me to run with them. as a regular" / "also, guess what. the big rvr group wants me with them every run"
- **A:** "oh? which group" / "wait, who asked you"
- **B:** "the one everyone talks about. they said i have good instincts" / "the big one. they said i pull clean. i wonder who taught me that"
- **A:** "huh. they never asked me" / "they didnt ask about me? i've been out there for ages"
- **E:** "lol they want the young blood {name_a}" / "sounds like they want {name_b}, not the teacher"
- **A:** "no, thats great. really. go" / "thats good. you should go {name_b}"
- **B:** "you could come too. i'll ask if they have room" / "i can ask them about you. they might have a spot"
- **A:** "nah. somebody has to watch the newbies. just bind first" / "no no. go have fun out there. just bind before you go"
- **C:** "was that a little sad or am i reading into it" / "{name_a} sounded a bit hurt there"
- **D:** "leave it. its a proud teacher moment" / "its fine. teachers are allowed to be proud"
- **F:** ""just bind first" is the most teacher thing ever said" / "bind first. dont pull adds. eat your vegetables"
- **B:** "ty {name_a}. i wont forget who taught me" / "i owe you everything {name_a}. really"
- **A:** "you owe me nothing. just dont die stupid out there" / "just come back in one piece"
- *(quiet for 6 seconds)*
- **G:** "famous last words. they always come back different" / "they all leave eventually. thats how it goes"

### story mentor 2

Levels 10–50 · 20 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (NoLifer), B (Leveler/Explorer/Socialite), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **H:** "dumb question. how do you pull just one from a group of three" / "how do people pull one mob without the whole pack coming"
- **B:** "easy. run in and hit the first one. the others are slow" / "just charge in and hit it. the adds wont follow far"
- **A:** "no. that gets people killed. pull from range and back off around a corner" / "uh, dont do that. ranged pull, back up around a corner, let it come to you"
- **B:** "thats the old way {name_a}. the group i run with now just goes in" / "nobody does the corner thing anymore. too slow"
- **A:** "the old way is the way that keeps people alive" / "slow is how you stay away from your bindstone"
- **H:** "so... which one do i do" / "ok. now im more confused than before"
- **G:** "do what {name_a} said. charging in is how you meet your bindstone" / "listen to {name_a}. i've seen a thousand charges end at the bind"
- **B:** "wow. so now i get corrected in front of the whole realm" / "cool. so everyone corrects me in faction now"
- **A:** "i wouldnt have to if you didnt teach it wrong" / "you answered wrong. i fixed it. thats all"
- **B:** "i'm {level}, {name_a}. i know how to pull" / "{level} and still being told how to pull. great"
- **E:** "ohhh the student talks back" / "the kid has a voice now"
- **A:** "you learned the careful stuff from me. now you're teaching the bad parts" / "you didnt learn charging in from me"
- **B:** "no, i learned it from people who actually win fights" / "i learned it out in {frontier} with real groups"
- **C:** "{name_b} hasnt grouped with {name_a} once since that invite" / "{name_b} has been running with that group nonstop since they asked"
- **A:** "i noticed" / "yeah. i noticed"
- **D:** "you two should just talk. in tells. not here" / "maybe take this to tells guys"
- **B:** "nothing to talk about. maybe i just dont need a teacher anymore" / "maybe i outgrew the lessons {name_a}"
- **A:** "...ok {name_b}. noted" / "wow. ok then"
- **F:** "the teacher just got benched" / "ouch. the student graduated themselves"
- **H:** "i'm doing the corner thing. i like living" / "i'll go with the corner. dying costs too much"

### story mentor 3

Levels 10–50 · 22 lines · about 3 min · roles: A (NoLifer), B (Leveler/Explorer/Socialite), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer)

- **G:** "so {name_b}'s new group called {name_a}'s way "old school and slow". in public" / "heard {name_b} and that big group joking that {name_a} plays like its launch week"
- **A:** "old school. the person i pulled out of the dirt calls me old school" / "i taught you where your hotbar is and now im old school"
- **B:** "i didnt say it like that. i said your way is slow. it is" / "it wasnt mean. i said you play safe. you do"
- **A:** "safe kept you alive the whole way up" / "safe is why you're not still dying outside town"
- **B:** "and now safe is holding me back" / "safe is boring {name_a}. i want to fight"
- **A:** "i bought your first decent armor. i ran you through every dungeon in the realm" / "who bought your first real armor {name_b}? who ran you through every dungeon?"
- **B:** "and i said thank you. in faction. remember?" / "i thanked you in front of the whole realm. what more do you want"
- **A:** "a little respect. and not acting like you did it alone" / "i want you to not act like you did it alone"
- **E:** "students leave. thats the whole point of teaching" / "come on {name_a}, the bird has to leave the nest some day"
- **G:** "theres leaving and theres spitting on the nest" / "leaving is fine. trashing your teacher in public isnt"
- **B:** "i didnt trash anyone! my group said it, i just laughed" / "it was a joke in group chat, not a speech"
- **A:** "you laughed. thats the part" / "you laughed along {name_b}. thats worse"
- **C:** "so the big group is laughing at {name_a} now? cold" / "the cool kids are making fun of {name_a}? harsh"
- **D:** "this is so sad. you two were the best duo in the realm" / "i liked these two together. this is awful"
- **B:** "you know what {name_a}, i never asked to be your project" / "i never asked to be babysat {name_a}"
- **A:** "babysat. ok. then you're on your own. dont send me tells when you're stuck" / "then good luck out there. no more tells for help"
- **B:** "fine by me" / "fine. i wont need them"
- **F:** "ok faction, pick a side. {name_a} or {name_b}" / "realm, whos right here? the teacher or the student"
- **G:** "{name_a}. respect the one who taught you" / "{name_a}. no question"
- **E:** "{name_b}. let them fly" / "{name_b}. teachers have to let go"
- **C:** "this ends with one of them face down in {frontier}, i just know it" / "someone is ending up at the bindstone over this"
- *(quiet for 6 seconds)*
- **D:** "i hope they make up. i really do" / "please make up you two"

### story mentor 4

Levels 10–50 · 19 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (NoLifer), B (Leveler/Explorer/Socialite), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **C:** "did everyone hear? {name_b}'s big group wiped in {frontier} and left them lying there" / "word is {name_b}'s new group wiped, logged, and left {name_b} dead out there"
- **B:** "it wasnt that bad" / "ok. it was pretty bad"
- **E:** "they just left you? some group" / "they left you lying there? classy"
- **B:** "they said "release and run back" and logged off. all of them" / "one of them typed "rel" and they were gone. every one"
- **A:** "and guess who went out there with a friend who can rez" / "someone had to go get them. i brought a friend who rezzes"
- **B:** "you came. after everything i said" / "{name_a} came out. after i said all that"
- **A:** "i said no more tells. i never said i'd leave you lying there" / "of course i came. thats what teachers do. even for ungrateful students"
- **D:** "ok that is the best thing i've read in faction" / "aww. i'm not crying, you're crying"
- **B:** "i'm sorry {name_a}. for the laughing. and for "babysat"" / "sorry for the old school thing. you were right about the pulling"
- **A:** "and i'm sorry i corrected you in front of everyone. you're not a kid anymore" / "i held on too tight. you've grown up out there"
- **G:** "look at that. two grown ups" / "finally, some sense in faction"
- **B:** "can we duo again? not lessons. just a duo" / "lets group again. as equals this time"
- **A:** "equals. ok. but i still pull" / "sure. equals. i still get to say "told you so""
- **F:** ""told you so" is the teacher's last weapon" / "the teacher always keeps one lesson back"
- **H:** "can one of you teach me? i still mess up the corner thing" / "so who teaches me now? i'm still pulling everything"
- **B:** "i will. {name_a} can grade me" / "i got this one. {name_a} can watch and wince"
- **A:** "deal. just dont teach the charging part" / "go ahead. i'll be right here correcting you"
- **E:** "the student becomes the teacher. classic" / "and round it goes. faction style"
- *(quiet for 6 seconds)*
- **H:** "ok. ranged pull, back up, corner. got it" / "pulled one. just one. i'm a genius"

### story mentor 4a

Levels 10–50 · 18 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (NoLifer), B (Leveler/Explorer/Socialite), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **A:** "big news. {name_b} sent me a tell and asked for lessons again" / "so {name_b} came back. asked to learn properly this time"
- **B:** "yeah. i did. i was wrong. the charging thing gets people killed" / "i'm back with {name_a}. turns out the slow way works"
- **C:** "did the big group finally drop you?" / "what happened to the cool group"
- **B:** "they kept wiping. every run. charging in. i left" / "four wipes in a row, all charging in. i'm done with them"
- **G:** "told you. you never forget who taught you" / "the old ways are old because they work"
- **A:** "and {player} said it in faction when nobody else would. ty {player}" / "{player} backed me when half the realm called me old school. thank you {player}"
- **B:** "yeah {player}, i read that. you were right. annoyingly" / "{player} said "listen to your teacher" and i hated it. and it was true"
- **E:** "{name_b} admitting it. never thought i'd see the day" / "the student came home. didnt see that coming"
- **B:** "lesson one again. ranged pull, back off, corner" / "ok. ranged pull, corner, let them come. i remember"
- **A:** "good. lesson two: pick groups that dont laugh at your friends" / "and lesson two: dont laugh when your group trashes your friends"
- **B:** "yeah. that one i learned the hard way" / "that one hurt to learn"
- **D:** "what a way for it to end" / "im so glad they made up"
- **F:** "{name_a} is going to be unbearable now" / "{name_a} gets to say "told you so" forever now"
- **A:** "only once a day. maybe twice" / "i'll keep it to once an hour"
- **H:** "so can i join the lessons?" / "room for one more student?"
- **A:** "sure. ask {name_b}. they're my assistant now" / "of course. {name_b} can show you. under supervision"
- **B:** "under supervision. great" / "i'm a teacher with a teacher. fine"
- **G:** "{player} for realm mentor" / "someone give {player} a student too"

### story mentor 4b

Levels 10–50 · 18 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (NoLifer), B (Leveler/Explorer/Socialite), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **B:** "news: i'm leading my own group now. small one. no charging" / "so i started my own group. they actually listen"
- **A:** "congrats {name_b}. really" / "i heard. well done {name_b}"
- **C:** "wait {name_a} is being nice about it?" / "no fight? {name_a} just said congrats?"
- **A:** "i had time to think. {name_b} outgrew me. thats what students are for" / "i held on too tight. you were ready and i wasnt"
- **B:** "{player} said it in faction: teachers have to let go. that stuck with me" / "ty {player}. you said i could stand on my own and i believed it"
- **A:** "yeah, {player}. you took the student's side. i was mad at you for a while" / "{player}, i was annoyed at you. you were right though, which is worse"
- **E:** "told you. let them fly" / "the bird flew. and didnt even crash"
- **B:** "and we use the corner pull. dont tell anyone" / "for the record, my group pulls around corners. its good"
- **A:** "i KNEW it" / "ha. so the old school way stuck"
- **B:** "some of it. the good parts" / "the parts that work, yes"
- **G:** "still think a little respect was owed" / "i still say {name_a} deserved more credit"
- **B:** "{name_a} gets credit. every new person in my group hears who taught me" / "everyone in my group knows where i learned it"
- **D:** "aww" / "thats lovely"
- **A:** "if your group ever needs someone old school, i'm one tell away" / "just tell me if you get stuck out there"
- **B:** "always. old school is welcome" / "you're always welcome {name_a}. you can even pull"
- **F:** "the teacher joins the student's group. full circle" / "now the teacher is the recruit lol"
- **H:** "room for me {name_b}? i finally pull one at a time" / "{name_b}, i do the corner thing now. can i come?"
- **B:** "sure. you learned from the best. well, second best" / "of course. {name_a} would be proud"

### story bounty 1

Levels 10–50 · 20 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (Scout/Hunter/Ranger), B (Scout/Hunter/Ranger), C (Leveler), D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **C:** "someone camp the mile gate in {frontier} please. a {enemy_mod} stealther keeps jumping people running out" / "that {enemy_mod} stealther is back at the mg in {frontier}. third time i ate the floor today"
- **F:** "the mile gate ganker strikes again" / "lol the mg ganker has a fan club"
- **D:** "ok thats it. bounty. gold to whoever drops that stealther first" / "bounty time. i'll pay whoever kills that ganker first. realm pride"
- **A:** "say no more. i'm a {class}. hunting stealthers is what i do" / "{class} here. i'll have that stealther on the ground in no time"
- **B:** "lol {name_a}, you couldnt find a stealther if it sat on your head. its mine" / "cute. {name_b} is already on it, {name_a}. go home"
- **A:** "{name_b}. of course" / "oh great, {name_b} wants the bounty too"
- **B:** "i want the bragging rights. the gold is a bonus" / "its not about gold. its about being first"
- **E:** "a race! first one to drop the ganker" / "two archers, one ganker. this is great"
- **G:** "stealthers always say they got the kill. i want proof" / "i'll believe it when someone sees the body"
- **A:** "i'll paste the kill line in faction. word for word" / "proof? i'll post the combat log"
- **B:** "and mine will be up before yours" / "i'll paste mine first"
- **H:** "i'll sit by the mile gate guards and keep score" / "i'll stand by the mg and watch who gets it"
- **D:** "rules: a clean kill. if the guards get it, nobody gets paid" / "the bounty is for a real kill. no guard kills, no zerg"
- **B:** "guards wont get near it. i'm faster" / "i dont need guards. or {name_a}"
- **A:** "you need glasses. you shot a tree last time we grouped" / "{name_b} spent a whole fight shooting at a rock once"
- **B:** "that rock moved!" / "it was a stealther shaped rock"
- **F:** "rofl stealther shaped rock" / "the rock had it coming"
- **C:** "i dont care who gets it, just make it stop" / "please. i just want to reach the frontier alive"
- **E:** "my money is on {name_b}" / "odds? i say {name_a}"
- *(quiet for 6 seconds)*
- **G:** "whoever it is, no log, no gold" / "log first. then we talk about gold"

### story bounty 2

Levels 10–50 · 21 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (Scout/Hunter/Ranger), B (Scout/Hunter/Ranger), C (Leveler), D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **A:** "done. dropped the mg ganker. clean. pay up" / "the mile gate stealther is dead. by my hand. pay up"
- **B:** "lol no. i had it at half health before you even showed up" / "that was MY kill. i opened on it, you just finished it"
- **A:** "you opened and missed. twice" / "your arrows hit the dirt {name_b}. twice"
- **B:** "they were warning shots" / "i was softening it up"
- **D:** "so who do i pay" / "uh, so who gets the bounty"
- **G:** "i said log. somebody paste the log" / "logs. both of you. now"
- **A:** "my shot, then "the stealther dies". clean" / "here: my arrow hits, then the stealther is dead. right there"
- **B:** "and right above your line is mine. i hit it first" / "and one line up: my arrow. first blood was mine"
- **H:** "i was at the gate. arrows from everywhere. no idea whose was last" / "i saw it. both of them shooting. it was a mess"
- **E:** "so its a tie? tie means double bounty right?" / "a tie lol. boring"
- **D:** "no. no clear kill, no gold" / "nope. a tie means nobody gets paid"
- **A:** "thats robbery. i got the killing blow" / "the last hit counts. everyone knows that"
- **B:** "the first hit counts. thats the rule for named mobs" / "first hit is how it works for mobs. why not for gankers"
- **F:** "are we really doing camp rules for enemy players" / "first hit, last hit, the stealther doesnt care"
- **C:** "uh. it was back at the gate right after. it got me again" / "did anyone notice the ganker was back an hour later"
- **A:** "of course its back. they come back. still my kill" / "it came back. players do. still mine"
- **B:** "so your kill did nothing. great work {name_a}" / "wow, a kill that lasted an hour. legend"
- **A:** "at least my arrows go where i point them" / "at least i landed mine"
- **G:** "new rule. the bounty is for whoever kills it twice" / "two kills, then i believe it"
- **D:** "fine. double bounty. two clean kills, clear logs" / "ok, new terms: two kills, nothing messy"
- **E:** "round two" / "game on again"

### story bounty 3

Levels 10–50 · 20 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (Scout/Hunter/Ranger), B (Scout/Hunter/Ranger), C (Leveler), D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **E:** "oh here we go" / "ohhh fighting words"

- **F:** "the hunters are hunting each other now" / "the ganker is just watching them fight. probably laughing"

- **H:** "keeping score again. like before" / "i'll keep score. again. i'm getting good at it"
- **A:** "fine. and when i get it you take back "liar". in faction" / "deal. i win, you say sorry for the liar thing"
- **B:** "and when i get it, you never paste another log" / "i win, you stop posting logs forever"
- **G:** "and if neither of you gets it?" / "what if the ganker gets you both"
- **A:** "wont happen" / "not a chance"
- **B:** "never" / "lol no"
- **E:** "faction, place your bets. {name_a} or {name_b}?" / "who are we backing, realm? {name_a} or {name_b}"
- **G:** "my gold is on the ganker" / "i back the stealther, honestly"

### story ballad 1

- **E:** "verse two when" / "more verses please"

- **A:** "some deaths are songs. this one was born to be sung" / "some deaths are just deaths. this one is art"

- **B:** "its been five minutes!!" / "HOW. it just got written"
- **A:** "good songs travel fast" / "thats the power of music"

- **B:** "{name_a} you take this song down" / "delete it. unsing it. whatever bards do"

- **B:** "the realm has spoken. {name_a} acts like a buffbot. too quiet" / "most of faction agrees with me. {name_a} is way too quiet"
- **A:** "fine. you want talk? i'll talk. all the time" / "i'm not a buffbot. but fine. i'll talk more. watch"
- **B:** "ty {player}. you saw it too. too quiet, too helpful" / "{player} backed me when everyone called me paranoid. ty {player}"
- **A:** "{player}... you think i'm a buffbot? really?" / "{player}, i'm hurt. i talk. look, i'm talking"
- **E:** "{player} said what we were all thinking" / "{player} saw it too"
- **A:** "hi realm. hows everyone. i'm talking now. buffs for anyone. what a day" / "hello everyone! just checking in! buffs available! also hi!"
- **C:** "oh no" / "what have we done"
- **A:** "{name_b}! hi! did you get good loot? i love loot. tell me about your loot" / "{name_b}, how was the hunting? nice frontier huh? lots of trees?"
- **B:** "ok stop" / "please stop"
- **A:** "but a buffbot would be quiet. so i'm talking. forever" / "i have to prove it. i'm going to talk every minute"
- **F:** "the buffbot broke" / "we made it chatty"
- **B:** "ok ok, you're not a buffbot. please go back to quiet" / "i take it back. you're a person. just be quiet again"
- **A:** "finally. buffs are back. quietly" / "ha. thank you. buffs are back"
- **D:** "everyone wins, sort of" / "best outcome"
- **G:** "lesson: be careful what you ask for" / "buffbots are real. this one just talks too much now"
- **H:** "buff please? no talking needed" / "can i get a buff? quietly?"
- **E:** "we broke the healer lol" / "the chattiest healer in the realm"
- **C:** "lesson learned. quiet is fine" / "never again will i call anyone quiet"

### story keepgrudge 1

Levels 35–50 · 21 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (Armsman/Paladin/Mercenary/Reaver/Warrior/Thane/Berserker/Savage/Hero/Champion/Blademaster), B (Wizard/Theurgist/Sorcerer/Cabalist/Runemaster/Spiritmaster/Eldritch/Enchanter/Mentalist), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **A:** "ok who told the casters to stay on the wall at {keep}. we nearly lost the inner door" / "we almost lost {keep} because the casters stood on the wall the whole fight"
- **B:** "i did. casters belong on the wall. thats where we hit them from" / "that was me. casters on the wall is how you hold a keep {name_a}"
- **A:** "the ram was on the door and you were up there throwing sparkles at their back line" / "the door was getting rammed and you were nuking people in the back"
- **B:** "those people in the back were their healers. you're welcome" / "i was killing their healers. thats why you lived"
- **C:** "i was at {keep}. it was close. really close" / "i was there. the door was nearly down"
- **A:** "we needed you inside the door. ae on the ram crew" / "you should have been inside, hitting whoever came through"
- **B:** "and get flattened by their whole zerg in a doorway? no" / "cast in a doorway, with the {enemy_mod} zerg on the other side? great plan"
- **E:** "melee against casters. the oldest fight in the frontier" / "here we go. tanks against casters"
- **A:** "we held because my group plugged the door" / "we only held because my group stood in the door"
- **B:** "you held because we killed everything that came through it" / "you held because our nukes landed"
- **D:** "it held. thats a win. both of you" / "you BOTH held it. thats the point. it held"
- **A:** "barely" / "by a hair"
- **B:** "and next time it holds better if the tanks dont run out the door" / "next time, dont charge out the front gate"
- **A:** "the ram was RIGHT THERE. someone had to kill it" / "i charged out because the ram was right there"
- **B:** "you died in three seconds and the ram didnt notice" / "and you died. and the ram was fine"
- **F:** "the ram did not notice lol" / "the ram sends its regards {name_a}"
- **A:** "at least i went for the ram. you watched from a wall" / "i fought. you watched"
- **B:** "some of us fight with our brains" / "i fought from a smart place"
- **G:** "i've seen this argument at every keep since launch" / "this happens every keep fight. melee want the door, casters want the wall"
- **E:** "so who's right though" / "ok but who's right"
- **H:** "the casters. obviously. they're alive" / "the melee. obviously. a keep is a door"

### story keepgrudge 2

Levels 35–50 · 20 lines · about 2 min · roles: A (Armsman/Paladin/Mercenary/Reaver/Warrior/Thane/Berserker/Savage/Hero/Champion/Blademaster), B (Wizard/Theurgist/Sorcerer/Cabalist/Runemaster/Spiritmaster/Eldritch/Enchanter/Mentalist), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **B:** "and you're a walking door" / "and you're a door with legs"
- **G:** "the enemy is going to walk right in while they argue" / "these two are going to lose {keep} arguing about {keep}"
- **H:** "i'm not going near the next {keep} defense" / "next attack on {keep} is going to be a mess"

### story loan 1

Levels 15–50 · 21 lines · about 3 min · roles: A (NoLifer/Crafter/Leveler), B (Daredevil/Socialite/Explorer), C, D (Socialite), E (Daredevil), F, G (NoLifer), H

- **A:** "{name_b}. been a while. the gold i lent you for your armor?" / "hey {name_b}, about that gold i lent you. remember?"
- **B:** "of course i remember. i'm working on it" / "yes yes. its coming. soon"
- **A:** "you said soon before" / "soon was a while ago {name_b}"
- **C:** "oh a loan" / "uh oh. money between friends"
- **G:** "never lend gold. ever. first rule of the realm" / "this is why you never lend coin. never"
- **B:** "its fine. {name_a} knows i'm good for it" / "{name_a} knows i pay. i always pay"
- **A:** "i'd believe it more with some gold in my purse" / "i'd know that better if i saw some of it"
- **B:** "i keep dying. repairs, potions, it adds up" / "repairs are killing me. dying costs a fortune"
- **E:** "weird, because {name_b} has a brand new dye job on that armor" / "but you have a fresh dye job"
- **A:** "wait. you dyed the armor. the armor my gold bought" / "you bought dyes?? for the armor i paid for?"
- **B:** "it was cheap dye. barely anything" / "dyes are cheap!"
- **A:** "cheap is not free! cheap is money! my money!" / "cheap or not, thats my gold you're wearing"
- **F:** "to be fair it is a nice color" / "the armor looks great though"
- **B:** "think of it as an investment in how i look" / "see? it looks great. you're investing in a good looking realm"
- **A:** "i want a return. in gold" / "i'm investing in getting my gold back"
- **D:** "give them time. {name_b} always comes through" / "{name_b} will pay. they're good people"
- **A:** "i've given time" / "time is all i've given"
- **B:** "you'll see some gold soon. promise promise" / "i'll start paying it back. i promise"
- **A:** "everyone heard that. faction is my witness" / "i'm holding you to that. in faction"
- **H:** "witnessed" / "i'm a witness"
- *(quiet for 6 seconds)*
- **G:** "never lend. thats all i'm saying" / "i'm just saying. never lend"

### story loan 2

- **B:** "i'm working on it!" / "i will!"
- **F:** "the silver drip is a strategy" / "this could take forever. i'm here for it"
- **H:** "witness reporting. a few silver changed hands" / "i'm still a witness by the way"

### tale thidranki

- **A:** "{name_b} you on? tell thidrand, the farm under the faste" / "{name_b}, do the winter nights one for the new ones"

- **B:** The hill where the Faste stands was a farm once, and the farmer's son was Thidrand. He was eighteen, and everyone liked him.
- **B:** At the winter nights feast there was a seer among the guests, and he said that nobody should go out that night, whoever knocked.
- **B:** Late, when the household was asleep on the benches, someone knocked at the door three times.
- **B:** Thidrand thought it shameful to leave a guest out in the cold, so he took his sword and went to the door.
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "who was it?" / "so who was knocking?"
- **B:** There was nobody at the door. But nine women in black were riding into the homefield from the north with their swords drawn.
- **B:** And from the south came nine more, in white, on white horses, riding as hard as they could.
- **B:** The ones in black reached him first. They found him at first light by the woodpile, and he lived long enough to say what he saw.
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "who were they?" / "so what were the ones in white?"
- **B:** The seer said both were spirits of the house. The black knew a change was coming, and would have their due before it came.
- **B:** The white belonged to what was coming. They had meant to save him, and they had been too late.
- **B:** The family left the hill in the spring. The tomte and the bwca stayed, and they have quarrelled over that farm ever since.
- **B:** Nobody who has fallen on that hill since has stayed dead. The ones in white are never late there now.
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "...going to leave porridge out for the tomte tonight" / "...never late there now. huh"
