### tale aidon-the-archwizard

Levels 1–50 · 20 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} who is the wizard on the stair in the hall of the corrupt" / "{name_b}, tell the aidon one"
- **C:** "hush all, academy story" / "quiet in faction, this ones about the archwizard"
- **D:** "go on {name_b}, my master wont say his name" / "please. the academy pretends he never existed"
- **B:** "alright, the academy one. once" / "sit down then. it runs long"
- **B:** aidon was the first mage of the academy to master all four. fire ice earth, and the theurgists' air
- **B:** magus cerwyn tested him. a brazier, a basin, a stone, an open window. she wrote she would not see it done again
- **B:** so the constable sent him to the pennine watch. to learn if the summoners' wards on the road could be broken
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "had nobody tried before" / "and he was the first to try?"
- **B:** magus idris tried before him. idris never came back
- **B:** aidon worked above the watch. two apprentices carrying candles up in boxes of a hundred
- **B:** and he learned what a ward is. a promise. every guardian stands there by a bargain. you pass a ward by answering it
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "so what did he answer?" / "and what did they ask him for?"
- **B:** the summoners wanted a keeper against each of the four ways a road can be broken. he gave them four of himself
- **B:** those are the reflections you fight. fire ice air earth. he can only pay again when all four are spent
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "which is why you leave one walking. every raid leader screams it" / "leave one up. i have yelled that a hundred times"
- **B:** magus alun brought him down that way. two companies, keeping him one self short
- **B:** the academy says he was deceived. the church says he sold the watch. the inconnu say they made an offer and waited
- **B:** his letters still come up the stair. calm ones. promising the constable he will close the road for good
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "one self short. thats horrible and i will remember it" / "so the fire around him is their seal. huh"
- **C:** "he still writes letters. that part gets me" / "promising to close the road. every time"

### tale lord-gildas

Levels 1–50 · 21 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} why does the lord at the bottom of the hall of the corrupt face us and not the summoners" / "{name_b}, the gildas one"
- **C:** "quiet, this is the penhallow story" / "hush. hall of the corrupt tale"
- **D:** "tell it {name_b}. my guild wiped to his poleaxe last week" / "please, i want to know who he was"
- **B:** "fine. but no heckling this time" / "ok, quiet then"
- **B:** gildas was second son of the house of penhallow. a squire at castle sauvage
- **B:** he once held a breach in its wall for a night and a day. because nobody had told him he could leave
- **B:** thats why the constable picked him for the pennine watch. he would hold the road and never act on his own judgment
- **B:** the letter of appointment called that his great virtue
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "sounds like a good soldier to me" / "whats wrong with that"
- **B:** twenty years nothing came up that road his watch could not turn back. the watch set its hours by his evening round
- **B:** then the realm sent aidon. and gildas trusted him because the realm had sent him. he overruled his own captain
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "and the order? theres always an order in these" / "so how did the watch end up dead at its posts"
- **B:** that last winter his men began wandering from their posts. so he gave the order. no man leaves his post for any reason
- **B:** the summoners only had to keep his order for him. forever
- **B:** thats why the dead watch cant be hurt a step past its posts. and why he is bound to the lowest point of the road
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "pull them off their post and you hit air. ask me how i know" / "learned that one the expensive way"
- **B:** he died there the last night. shield up, facing the summoner's hall. waiting for visitors who were already behind him
- **B:** press him hard and he takes up the penhallow poleaxe. his family says you do that when only honour is left to guard
- **B:** his grand niece dame morwen led the first company to bring him down. she goes back every spring until he knows her
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "sorry, was afk. what happened with the grand niece" / "did i miss the end? phone rang"
- **C:** "every spring. gods" / "i always go quiet at the grand niece"

- **C:** "shh, krondon story" / "quiet all, the ogre queen"
- **D:** "yes please. i got thrown into a fire and nobody explained" / "go on {name_b}, the krondon one"
- **B:** "ha, ok. sit down" / "alright, the krondon one. settle in"
- **B:** orylle was born in the undercroft. the deepest seam of the ogres who dug under the isles before the hall folk built on them
- **B:** she was the first of her people to climb up and look at what they had been digging under
- **B:** she found krondon dying of age. its last elder counting empty chairs at the high table
- **B:** she took the hall in one night without breaking a door. the elder died asking if she came as guest or enemy
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "and which was it" / "did she answer him"
- **B:** then she walked every room. a ghost, the last teacher, translated the titles over the doors
- **B:** and she gave every title to an ogre. the stores, the horn, the school, the road. thats why they all have work
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "but the throwing?" / "and the fire?"
- **B:** hall folk guest law. a stranger who came in unannounced was set back on the guest stone to begin again properly
- **B:** she hurls challengers down her hall onto that stone. into the fire burning on it. and fights only those who walk back
- **B:** orshom brong drank the great hearth the night she took the hall. she made him champion and consort
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "kill one and the other roars the name. loudest thing in albion" / "when one falls the other screams it. you dont forget"
- **B:** sir cador reached her first with twenty and was swept out. he came back with fifty chosen for stubbornness
- **B:** and ice mages for the fire on the stone. they walked back every time. with the stone cold she throws no one
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "noted. i stand well away from the fire next time" / "ok but where do you stand so you land somewhere safe"
- **C:** "they say she wants neither to serve nor to rule. only to keep" / "guest or enemy. she never did answer"

### tale duraek-the-empowered

Levels 1–50 · 21 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} whats wrong with the drakoran lord in avalon city. fire heals him??" / "{name_b}, tell the one about dura'ek"
- **C:** "quiet please, avalon city story" / "hush, {name_b} is telling the empowered"
- **D:** "please. our wizard nuked him back to full last night" / "go on, i need to know what we did wrong"
- **B:** "sure. ears open" / "fine, fine. listen up"
- **B:** dura'ek was one war lord among five when avalon burned. lord of the dry'ak, the clan that holds doors
- **B:** one thing set him apart. he kept more sworn protectors than any other lord. every one bound to die before he does
- **B:** morgana gave his clan the doors. he took the chancellor's court at the heart of the city
- **B:** a woman saw him standing on the steps there. listening to the paving
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "listening to what" / "whats under the paving"
- **B:** under it lay the stair down to the crystal cave. and what sleeps there
- **B:** on the third night the readers saw a cold light rise from the city. and he went down alone
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "and came back how?" / "what did the dragon do to him?"
- **B:** nobody agrees. the clans say xanxicar gave him a gift. his own captured men say he stole from a sleeper
- **B:** the readers say he only touched something the dragon lay on. he came up at dawn burning
- **B:** xanxicar has two heads and no breath. dura'ek has two breaths now. fire and frost feed him
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "no fire no frost. say it with me" / "NO FIRE NO FROST. every pull"
- **B:** thats the cry sir kenrick brought him down to. and his six protectors died before him. as they had sworn
- **B:** he forbids the dragon's name in his court. he wants to be a lord in his own right
- **B:** and every sentry on the stair below says it anyway
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "so our wizard healed him. great" / "no fire no frost. writing it on my hand"
- **C:** "he forbids the name and they all say it. i like that bit" / "listening to the paving. brr"

### tale green-knight

Levels 1–50 · 22 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} you about? tell the green knight one" / "{name_b}, the knight up in sauvage. whats his story"
- **C:** "shh all, {name_b} is doing sauvage" / "quiet in faction a sec, green knight story"
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "the one where nobody whispers to him. i know it" / "here we go, defend it defend it"
- **D:** "please {name_b}, i ride past him every day and never knew" / "go on, i only know hes green and big"
- **B:** alright alright. find a stump and sit
- **B:** north edge of forest sauvage, where the road climbs to the pennines. thats where he stands
- **B:** the woodsmen say he was there before the castle. the bards say before arthur
- **B:** he greets everyone who stops. warns them the forest turns on those who wake it
- **B:** and then he waits. he has never drawn on anyone who did not ask
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "ask how?" / "so you have to start it?"
- **B:** you whisper to him. defend it. old hopcyn cut wood there forty years and saw three men ask
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "and what happens when you ask?" / "ok so someone asks. then what?"
- **B:** the deadwood stands up. a rotten felwood tears out of the ground at every mans feet
- **B:** so forty of you fight forty trees as well as him
- **B:** his sword sets the air on fire round whoever it hits. stand at that mans shoulder and you burn too
- **B:** and he knows who keeps a company standing. now and then he walks past the tanks to cut at a healer
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "guard your clerics. every time" / "yeah he loves a cleric"
- **B:** hurt him and he breaks off. walks to another clearing, never the same one twice running
- **B:** takes a breath there and heals a little. only a little. follow him and keep cutting
- **B:** leave him alone a minute though and hes whole. bows to the empty road and the trees lie down
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "wait so who whispers? not me" / "i am never stopping on that road again"
- **C:** "and the bards still think hes owed a blow from the old gawain story" / "ok but who here has actually been thrown in with forty trees"

### tale coliath

Levels 1–50 · 21 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} whats coliath actually guarding on gwyddneau? you know it" / "{name_b}, tell the lich on the height"
- **C:** "oh this is a good one. hush" / "everyone quiet, coliath story"
- **D:** "yes please, i only ever saw it from the lammia town" / "tell it {name_b}, nobody i ask agrees"
- **B:** ok. sit. this one has three endings
- **B:** coliath stands on the highest ground of gwyddneau. above the north end of the lammia town
- **B:** its one of morgana's liches. a dead mage she kept past its death
- **B:** it has never left that height. not to chase, not to feed. not once since the first year of her war
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "so whats up there?" / "guarding what though?"
- **B:** thats the oldest wager in the isle of glass garrison. the garrison says treasure. soldiers always do
- **B:** the lammia of clifton say a drowned country's king is buried under it
- **B:** and the lacula sept say it guards nothing but itself. a lich keeps its death close
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "and the dead round it? where did they come from" / "does it stand alone up there?"
- **B:** no. when it came, the ancient dead climbed out of the drowned ground. they stood round it
- **B:** they turn with it when anyone comes up the slope. break them and the height stays bare till it comes back
- **B:** and when it dies they all drop with it
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "and the end is the bad part" / "wait for the drinking. thats where we wiped"
- **B:** the lacula have a saying. a lich near its end stops striking and starts drinking
- **B:** low on life it drops its arms. then it drains whoever is in front of it. follows them anywhere
- **B:** everything it drinks it keeps. the lammia say be quicker than its thirst
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "so which one is it, the king or the lich itself?" / "nobody ever settled it?"
- **C:** "the lacula bury their dead facing the height. to watch if anyone ever is quicker" / "i still say treasure"

### tale spirit-of-lord-emthoro

Levels 1–50 · 20 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b}, the ghost lord on inishail. tell it" / "{name_b} tell us about emthoro, the one the orcs serve"
- **C:** "hush for {name_b}, inishail story" / "quiet all. emthoro"
- **D:** "please, i got "immune to your damage" on him and gave up" / "go on {name_b}, i want to know why nothing hits him"
- **B:** right. ears open
- **B:** lord emthoro held inishail before the war. the colleges lost his name, or never wrote it down
- **B:** he held the isle the old way. by standing on it
- **B:** he walked his bounds. heard his tenants at his own door
- **B:** and no order counted unless the sender stood on his ground first
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "even the colleges?" / "so messengers had to walk up to him?"
- **B:** the college of the old tongues sent messengers twice. both walked up from the shore. they wrote it down, annoyed
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "so did morgana raise him?" / "how did he end up dead there?"
- **B:** no. the war reached the isle and he was standing before his door. he is standing there now
- **B:** the readers at caer gothwaite think her war did not make him. the only one
- **B:** and his old way still holds. from beyond his ground nothing reaches him. arrows, spells, stones
- **B:** you have to come up and stand there. and then something goes out of all of you. into him
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "and the more of you up there the more he drinks" / "pack in close and he heals off the lot of you"
- **B:** he wont step off to follow anyone. a lord does not chase his tenants. they come to him
- **B:** and they do. the dead of the isle and the orcs who fled krondon. his tenants now, unasked
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "so the orcs work for a ghost and never signed anything" / "i would read stolen books too"
- **D:** "the lammia say you go up and stay till he leaves. thats the only way out of a tenancy" / "ok, everyone stands on his ground. got it"

## Hibernia (99)

### tale murdaigean

Levels 1–50 · 18 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} are you on? tell the murdaigean one, the knots" / "{name_b}, do the run through murd for the new ones"
- **C:** "whisht everyone, {name_b} is telling the wood" / "quiet in faction please, the murdaigean one"
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "the knots story? go on then" / "here we go, braid your hair everyone"
- **D:** "go on {name_b}, i keep seeing those cords on the trees" / "please {name_b}, i never knew what the knots were"
- **B:** Long before the realm war the Fianna tried their young in that wood, and the dun at the heart of it was where the trial ended.
- **B:** They braided your hair and gave you the start of one tree, and then the whole company came after you through the wood.
- **B:** If they caught you, you had failed. If a braid came loose on a branch, or a dry stick cracked under your foot, you had failed.
- **B:** You had to leap a branch as high as your brow, go under one as low as your knee, and pull a thorn from your foot at a run.
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "did anyone ever reach the dun?" / "so who made it to the fort?"
- **B:** Hardly anyone. So wherever they caught you they tied a knot in a cord on the nearest tree, to show how far you had come.
- **B:** A boy who was caught halfway would come back as a grown man, and find his knot, and stand under it a while without a word.
- **B:** The Fianna went away a long time ago. The cords stayed on the trees, and the spiders took them over, one knot at a time.
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "wait, the webs are the cords?" / "so the webs in murd are the old knots?"
- **B:** Some of them. On the oldest trees, now and then, there is a knot in a web where no spider would ever tie one.
- **B:** The name of the dun says fortress twice in the old tongue. The filidh say that was so a runner who reached it would believe it.
- **B:** It was never built to keep anyone out. It was the one place in that wood where nobody was chasing you.
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "...going to look for knots next time" / "...i always thought those were just webs"
- **A:** "ty {name_b}, lovely telling" / "thanks {name_b}, i never knew that one"

- **E:** "huh, i thought bards only did the speed at the stables" / "i only ever see bards running speed near the stablemaster"

- **C:** "i change songs when the fight starts, the group lives" / "the speed comes down when the pull lands, its not hard"

- **A:** "first night ever, standing in mag mell. what now?" / "brand new to the game, standing in mag mell, where do i start"
- **B:** "welcome! stay around mag mell until things there go green" / "welcome. stay near mag mell for a few levels, then follow the road"
- **C:** "talk to the npcs in town too, some of them have tasks" / "read the quest givers in town, easy xp and some gear"
- **D:** "and say hi in faction any time, we dont bite" / "glad to have you, ask anything you want"
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **A:** "ok next question, what do the colors on monster names mean" / "what do the con colors mean, everything here is a different color"
- **B:** "green and blue are your friends, yellow is a fair fight, orange is a risk" / "grey gives nothing, green is easy, blue is good, yellow is even"
- **E:** "red and purple are where the fun is" / "red and purple are for brave people"
- **B:** "red and purple are how you meet your bind point" / "dont listen to that, red means run"
- **D:** *(sometimes)* "that one has died more than anyone in hibernia" / "spoken by someone who learned it the hard way. many times"
- *(quiet for 30 seconds)*
- **A:** "and when the things around mag mell go green, where then" / "where do i go once mag mell stops giving xp"
- **C:** "lough derg. the lough wolves and orchard nippers are perfect for you" / "head into lough derg, plenty of lough wolves at your level"
- **B:** "careful with the lough wolf cadgers, they hit harder" / "the cadgers are tougher than the regular wolves, watch the names"
- **A:** "something called a water beetle just bit me" / "whats a water beetle and why is it chasing me"
- **E:** "thats lunch, hit it back" / "hit it back harder"
- *(quiet for 20 seconds)*
- **D:** "which class are you going for?" / "what did you roll by the way"
- **A:** "{class}, for now anyway" / "{class}, still learning what that means"
- **D:** "nice pick, hibernia needs more of those" / "good choice, you will have fun"
- **C:** "after lough derg, connacht has plenty of room to hunt" / "connacht next, lots of space and lots of feccans"
- **B:** "low teens, silvermine mts. walking rocks and badgers give good xp" / "then silvermine mts, the walking rocks are slow and worth it"
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "and the luricaduanes. they look friendly. theyre not" / "beware the luricaduane, small but rude"
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **A:** "wow, this is way more help than i expected" / "youre all so helpful, thank you"
- **D:** "we were all new once" / "thats hibernia for you"
- **B:** "one more tip, visit your trainer every time you level" / "dont forget the trainer when you ding, its easy to forget"
- **A:** "will do. back to the wolves" / "off to lough derg, wish me luck"
