### tale avartack-the-champion

Levels 1–50 · 22 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} who is the champion before the hall in marfach" / "{name_b} tell avartack tonight"
- **C:** "hush, breifine story" / "quiet in faction, the ferryman's son"
- **D:** "please. he would not die and i want to know why" / "tell it {name_b}, the ford and all"
- **B:** "very well. gather in, the ford is a long tale" / "i will, if you all hush"
- **B:** avartack was the son of aed dub. the ferryman who kept the ford of the erne
- **B:** he grew up fighting in the water. where a man cannot step back or aside. only reach
- **B:** he was fostered beside the boy who became caimheul. the day caimheul was made chieftain he named his foster brother champion
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "so they grew up together?" / "brothers then, near enough?"
- **B:** twenty years he held the fords of breifine in single combat. never lost in the water
- **B:** he kept the law of the water. the ford belongs to whoever faces it. and a turned back may be struck down without shame
- **B:** his oath was this. i will stand before you until you have no need of me
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "thats a fine oath. isnt it?" / "whats wrong with the oath?"
- **B:** it has no word in it to end the watch
- **B:** the day caimheul died on the reed flats, avartack was away. holding the ford of the erne
- **B:** he came home to the bier. went under the hill first. and stood at his lord's feet in the grave chamber, facing the door
- **B:** he died on his feet in the third night. so on the fourth, when it happened, he did not need to stand up
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "and he is whole again the moment nobody stands against him" / "someone always faces him. always"
- **B:** he takes the floor before the hall for the ford. the first company came at him in a ring and broke at the back
- **B:** echna of ardee, a ferryman's daughter, faced him and never turned. her sister on her bank, her company at his sides
- **B:** when he fell she said the old word that ends a watch. it did not hold. but the ferrymen call it over the water at night
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "gotta log, early start. good one" / "ok. i am never turning my back in marfach"
- **C:** "he did not need to stand up. every time" / "they call it over the water. i like that they still try"

### tale chieftain-caimheul

Levels 1–50 · 23 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} tell the one about the chieftain in marfach" / "{name_b}, caimheul?"
- **C:** "quiet, this ones ours" / "hush. the crooked mouth"
- **D:** "go on. my grandfather called him ours and wouldnt say more" / "the chieftain, {name_b}. please"
- **B:** "our own chieftain. very well" / "gather in, then. quietly"
- **B:** caimheul ruled the breifine hills before there were keeps on them
- **B:** thats not the name his mother gave him. a lake man's knife left his mouth crooked and the hills forgot the other
- **B:** he was a hard lord. burned booleys, kept hostages. his people loved him anyway
- **B:** because no reaver took a cow from his valleys that he did not take back twice over
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "so a cattle lord?" / "hard how?"
- **B:** he taught his sons to fight with a stick in the dust of the yard
- **B:** the man in front meets the shield and reels. the man who wont reel he mocks until he comes on careless
- **B:** and the man at his side, where he cannot see. that one he kills first. with the long spear
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "and the hostages?" / "what happened on the reed flats?"
- **B:** he took nine hostage children of the lakes. when the peace broke they died of a coughing sickness in his hostage house
- **B:** only a keener in red sang for them
- **B:** years on, on the reed flats, a herd boy named mochta threw a spear into his side. his sister had been one of the nine
- **B:** his champion was away that day. holding a ford
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "stand at his flank and you die first. the verse says so" / "never at his side. front or nowhere"
- **B:** he was sung back up and does not hear the voice below at all. he takes every stranger for the lake men
- **B:** crochnat of howth heard the old yard verse at the muster. drew something in the dust. and brought him down the next night
- **B:** when he casts the shield aside he is hurt deep and thinks you are about to break. be ready then
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "he holds their road because he thinks its his country" / "so he doesnt even know. hm"
- **C:** "the keeners added a verse. asking him to find mochta and the nine and say he is sorry" / "he was ours. thats the hard part"

### tale balor

Levels 1–50 · 21 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b} tell balor. the real one not the nursery one" / "{name_b} whats up in tur suil above the vale"
- **C:** "hush, the eye" / "quiet all, tur suil"
- **D:** "please. i know lugh and the stone. what came after" / "{name_b} tell the second verse"
- **B:** "the second verse. very well" / "if you will be quiet, yes. not the nursery one"
- **B:** you know the first verse. king of the fomorians. an eye poisoned by his father's druids cauldron that destroyed whatever it saw
- **B:** he shut his daughter away on tory against a prophecy. her son lugh drove the eye out through his skull at mag tuired
- **B:** it fell facing his own host. and destroyed it
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "everyone knows that part" / "and thats the end?"
- **B:** the sylvan know a second verse. the eye's bearers wrapped it in seven hides and carried it west. walking backwards
- **B:** the fomorians say it followed its people of its own will
- **B:** on the shore of hy brasil the hides slipped. it looked on a valley for one breath
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "gleann glas?" / "what was in the valley?"
- **B:** a sylvan village. gleann glas. it burned, and everything else in the valley. thats the vale of balor
- **B:** on the crag above they built tur suil around it. one window
- **B:** and grew a king around the eye again. with a household
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "his hide turns everything and he wont bleed outside his chamber" / "and then the roof opens. you will see"
- **B:** as he weakens an eye opens in the roof, beyond any weapon. it chooses one of those below and burns them until it shuts
- **B:** eochaidh of ardee was chosen at the last. fiachna of howth brought the king down the next spring
- **B:** carrying a smooth stone for lugh's sake. every company that climbs tur suil carries one since
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "i am finding a smooth stone tomorrow" / "it chooses one. great"
- **C:** "walls do not hold everything. thats how the book ends" / "and the vale glows red more often. they say"

- **A:** he thinks that floor is a ford. law of the water. a turned back may be struck down
- **A:** the second nobody is fighting him, hes back to full

### fireside balor

### tale evern

Levels 1–50 · 20 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b}, tell evern. the stones in breifine" / "{name_b} the fairy lord one, please, for the new folk"
- **C:** "gather in, {name_b} is telling evern" / "hush, breifine"
- **D:** "go on. i walk past his ring on every keep run" / "yes {name_b}, all of it"
- **B:** very well. come in near the stones
- **B:** evern was standing in breifine when the frontier was only hills
- **B:** the keeps were built round his ring of stones because nobody could move them. or him
- **B:** the sylvan think he is a great lord of the fairy kind. gone strange with age
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "then why is he still here?" / "did he choose to stay?"
- **B:** the druids think his ring was a gate once. he stayed to keep it after it closed
- **B:** the little wraith fairies of the frontier are his last subjects
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "and the fairies, what do they do in a fight?" / "how do the fairies help him?"
- **B:** when he is hurt they drift in on the wind. two, three, five of them
- **B:** they gather by his stones and pour healing into him. a great deal at a time
- **B:** so the fairies die first. always the fairies
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "and dont drag him off the ring" / "pull him off his stones and nothing lands"
- **B:** away from his ring he cannot be hurt at all. hits just slide off
- **B:** and let him alone a little while. he is whole and the fairies are gone
- **B:** under breifine run the marfach caves and the road to the summoners
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "so is he sitting on something?" / "hold on, whats under breifine then"
- **C:** "the old frontier hands think so. nobody has lifted him off it to check" / "kill the fairies first. i am writing that down"

### tale juggernaut

Levels 1–50 · 19 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b}, what is the juggernaut? the thing in the cursed forest" / "{name_b} tell the juggernaut story"
- **C:** "quiet, {name_b} is telling the cursed forest one" / "everyone hush a moment"
- **D:** "please. even the trees move for him" / "go on {name_b}, i never knew what he was"
- **B:** yes, if you let me finish this time
- **B:** nobody knows what he was before he walked the cursed forest
- **B:** he has no court. no servants. no tricks
- **B:** he walks his long road through the rotten wood. walking trees, blights and dark men all get out of his way
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "why do they move?" / "even the unseelie?"
- **B:** the seelie and the unseelie quarrel about everything. they agree on one thing. give him the road
- **B:** the sylvan think he is the broken bargain itself. given a body so neither court has to hold it
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "so how do you fight a bargain?" / "what is the trick to him then?"
- **B:** there is none. he just hits harder than anything his size should
- **B:** and he follows you a long way down his road. then he turns for home
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "run and he keeps coming. trust me" / "i tried kiting him once. once"
- **B:** warriors swear the colours lie about him. the scholars say the colours are right and the warriors are scared
- **B:** both may be true
- **B:** when he falls the whole forest holds its breath. to see if the debt is paid
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "and is it?" / "so is it ever paid"
- **A:** "never is. he comes back. thank you {name_b}" / "he always comes back. lovely telling, {name_b}"

### tale curengkur

Levels 1–50 · 20 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b}, the thing the mantids whisper about in allta fearann. tell it" / "{name_b} tell curengkur. the nest one"
- **C:** "softly now. {name_b} has the wild land story" / "hush, allta fearann"
- **D:** "please, i want to know why the bugs go quiet" / "go on {name_b}, all of it"
- **B:** come closer then. this one is told low
- **B:** allta fearann is the wild land. high country north of vigilant rock
- **B:** the mantids held it before the sylvan named it. a hard busy people. not friends, not enemies
- **B:** they are not afraid of much. they are afraid of curengkur
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "what is it?" / "why are they scared?"
- **B:** it hunts them where they nest. and it has a nest of its own
- **B:** a hollow on the high ground. the air above it burns
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "what is in the nest?" / "so what does the nest do?"
- **B:** when curengkur is roused the nest wakes with it. the air round it sears whoever stands there. over and over
- **B:** and curengkur spits acid. it hangs round its prey and eats through shell and skin
- **B:** it strikes from further off than any mantid can reach too
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "and it heals on the nest. fast" / "fight it at home and you lose"
- **B:** the mantids' oldest warning. curengkur is never wounded at home. a hurt that would kill it closes on the nest
- **B:** so their old hunters go up, sting it and run. draw it down the slope till the air is cool. then turn
- **B:** most of them die doing it. the mantids say its still better than the nest
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "so we pull it off the nest. got it" / "drag it away then fight. ok"
- **C:** "when it dies you can hear the mantids clicking all over the high country. for a while" / "if the mantids go quiet, turn round"

### tale marfoirwort

Levels 1–50 · 21 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b}, the killer plant in cothrom gorge. tell it" / "{name_b} tell marfoirwort, the bare ring one"
- **C:** "hush all, {name_b} has the gorge story" / "quiet, marfoirwort"
- **D:** "please, i did writhing death and never learned about the big one" / "tell it {name_b}"
- **B:** softly then, this one is old
- **B:** marfoirwort is the oldest thing growing in cothrom gorge. the gorge sylvan named it the killer plant
- **B:** they were not being poetic
- **B:** it stands at the west head of the gorge. in a wide ring of bare ground
- **B:** nothing has grown in that ring for an age. everything that ever grew there, it drank
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "what does it live on then?" / "no soil?"
- **B:** it lives on what walks near it
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "so how does it kill?" / "what happens when it hits you?"
- **B:** it strikes one of you. and the life goes out of everyone standing close round that one
- **B:** all that strength goes into marfoirwort. closes its own wounds
- **B:** so the oldest custom of the gorge. a sylvan crossing near the ring crosses alone
- **B:** one throat is a meal. five throats together feed it for a season
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "and every captain still stands too close" / "spread out. please. spread out"
- **B:** third summer of the isles, swords from tir na nog came to burn it
- **B:** they stood shoulder to shoulder round it. the way a good company stands round anything
- **B:** it drank from all of them every strike. whole by evening. the captain planted a rowan for the dead
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "shoulder to shoulder. ouch" / "so the trick is just dont stand together?"
- **C:** "and the gorge folk watch the ring for the day it starts to widen" / "cronwort on the east floor worries me now"

### tale mollunero

Levels 1–50 · 19 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b}, the cold mother at worlds end. tell it" / "{name_b} tell mollunero, the pool one"
- **C:** "hush, worlds end story" / "everyone quiet, {name_b} has the octonoid"
- **D:** "please, the fishers wont say her name" / "go on {name_b}, i want the grandmothers version too"
- **B:** yes. i will name her, though the fishers wont
- **B:** mollunero. strongest of the three mothers on the world's end shore
- **B:** she lies in the deep pool below the cliffs on the south shore
- **B:** the sea comes in through a gap there and stays cold in high summer. the fishers say the cold is hers
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "so its her making it cold?" / "how cold?"
- **B:** fishing lines that touch her come up stiff with frost
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "and the grandmothers story?" / "what do the grandmothers say?"
- **B:** that she lets her brood fight for her. she comes out when every child is dead
- **B:** coll of the domnann guides has seen her come up. he says thats a story for grandchildren
- **B:** she comes up with her young. seven or eight of them, near as heavy as a man
- **B:** the story has one true thing in it. the children never outlive her. she sinks, they sink
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "and whoever she holds goes grey" / "the cold blast on the tank. ugh"
- **B:** her arms are the worst. the cold of the pool goes into whoever she holds, again and again
- **B:** the fishers say warm him faster than she cools him. and no charm holds her
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "so heal the tank and ignore the grandmothers. ok" / "warm him faster. thats the whole fight"
- **C:** "the fomorians left her twice the catch they left balere, fiachra of howth wrote" / "i will never fish that shore again"

### tale myrddraxis

Levels 1–50 · 22 lines · about 3 min · roles: A, B, C (Socialite/Explorer), D, E (NoLifer/Daredevil), F (Leveler/Crafter/Explorer)

- **A:** "{name_b}, the hydra under fomor. tell the whole thing" / "{name_b} tell myrddraxis. the egg one"
- **C:** "gather round, {name_b} has the hydra" / "hush, fomor story"
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "five heads and all of them hungry. i know" / "here comes the egg"
- **D:** "please {name_b}, i heard the heads heal each other" / "go on, what is it really"
- **B:** well then. it was an egg once
- **B:** grey green and warm. a hydrar found it digging the lowest cistern of fomor, in the black sand
- **B:** the fomorians call it a gift of the sea. the sylvan call it a theft from the deep places
- **B:** the druids of tir na nog say an ollpheist. a great water worm, like the lough derg tales
- **F:** *(sometimes)* "how did it get five heads?" / "was it always that big?"
- **B:** it hatched with one head and grew five. one hunger with five mouths
- **B:** whatever any mouth tears from you mends the great body. and the other mouths a little
- **B:** it never leaves its mound. step out of its reach and it burns you where you stand
- *(quiet for 8 seconds)*
- **D:** "so how did anyone ever kill it?" / "what does it do when you hurt it?"
- **B:** hurt it deep and it bellows. the whole cistern stands still and stares
- **B:** before the great breath it draws in till the water sinks. then fire across everything
- **B:** the hydrars sing it. the beast draws in, the wise go out
- **B:** and between breaths one head looks at one of you. gathering fire slow enough to see
- **E:** *(sometimes)* "so walk away from your friends" / "the one it looks at walks off. always"
- **B:** rionach of tir na nog's company cut the mouths one at a time. when the small heads hung in the water the body could not mend
- **B:** the earth lurched. a light on the mound, like where a dragon falls
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **F:** "so it counts as a dragon?" / "a dragon with no wings. huh"
- **C:** "when balor's eye opens all the way, the hydrars will let it swim east" / "a scale is a closed door. bring a key, not a song"
